A player may switch channel using the chat panel **NEARBY / WORLD** button. New `nearby-chat` packets go through the same trusted Worker auth, sender identity binding, message normalization and 2.5s cooldown but are delivered only to spawned room peers within **20 meters** of the sender's movement-gated presence; sender gets actual server echo. This proximity is **COSMETIC only** and must never authorize PvP, loot, reach or combat damage. Validated incoming nearby packets create a 5-second overhead text-only speech sprite on a known remote player's avatar when in range, with two short lines, 76-character preview and texture/material cleanup on leave, expiration and replacement; full text stays in chat log. Global World Chat doesn't create speech bubbles.

## GAME LATE MULTIPLAYER STARTUP RECOVERY & VISIBLE CONNECTION STAGES (2026-11-18)

- **Baseline verified on current `main`:** [Pages #44296886894](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44296886894) SUCCESS; [PR #645 postmerge Jarrell's Peak Gameplay Smoke #44296599533](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44296599533) SUCCESS; [postmerge Browser Smoke #44296599557](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44296599557), [Validate #44296599510](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44296599510), and [Game CDN #44296599559](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44296599559) SUCCESS. Owner's two authenticated real players seeing each other, movement and World Chat remains **NOT VERIFIED**, so offline CI alone is not a customer-acceptance PASS.
- **Concrete Game-only startup dead-end:** The `client-presence.js` boot checks for separate CDN-loaded Supabase Game client, 3D peer adapter and player state for 60 × 500ms (=30s), and previously **returned permanently** when they were still missing. A very slow Android/WebGL boot or delayed Supabase provider could become ready after 30s yet never initiate multiplayer or World Chat without a user reload. This is a source-confirmed liveness gap; it is not yet proven to be the specific cause of owner's browser failure.
- **Fix in this branch:** Retain the fast 30-second startup retry phase; after that, display `MULTIPLAYER · STILL LOADING GAME & LOGIN · RETRYING` and continue probing the same approved first-party dependencies once every 3 seconds while the Game page is open, stopping at `pagehide`. When the real auth/3D adapter arrives, continue into the unchanged registered-only room ticket and protected Sanctuary spawn flow. No fake player, socket, chat or auth token introduced; the Worker still owns server admission. Stalled/absent CDN remains visibly slow and may still require a page reload for a permanently failed script.
- **In-game stage clarity:** During legitimate room connection, replace the previous dataset-only (often stale visible badge) with accurate badge text: `CHECKING GAME LOGIN`, `VERIFYING ROOM ACCESS`, `CONNECTING TO WORLD`, `VERIFYING SANCTUARY SPAWN`; continue `JOINING WORLD` after trusted welcome and `ONLINE` **only after server `spawn-accepted`**. This allows nontechnical two-browser QA to report the failure stage safely without passwords, JWTs, accounts, browser network logs or DevTools.
- **Regression tests:** Added a deterministic simulated 30s+ Game dependency load test (no ticket or chat before real components) that eventually joins through verified ticket and `spawn-accepted`; added phase-by-phase test proving ticket/socket/spawn statuses never prematurely create World Chat; updated prior wait/ticket tests to expect truthful new stages. Updated only Game page versioned script to `v=20261118-slow-boot-7` to prevent cached old behavior.
- **Scope/status:** Game-only client, Game page, client tests and this record; **branch pending PR, CI and Pages** at this record. No changes to Game Worker, PvP damage, loot, theft, grab, DEX, Swap, Launchpad, ClimbX. Existing registered-only Game room config stays enabled for approved `https://rooms.climboria.fun/`.
- **Exact next:** (1) Open PR, run exact-head Game Gateway security tests, Validate and Jarrell's Peak Game smoke; fix failures, merge, confirm Pages deployment. (2) On two genuinely distinct registered Game sessions at `https://climboria.fun/game/play.html`, verify both authentic `ONLINE`, visible real hero GLBs/Adventurer fallback, movement replication and bidirectional World Chat. If blocked, distinguish `LOADING GAME`, `CHECKING GAME LOGIN`, `VERIFYING ROOM ACCESS`, `CONNECTING TO WORLD`, `VERIFYING SANCTUARY SPAWN`, `SIGN IN REQUIRED`, `SAME ACCOUNT IN ANOTHER TAB`, `RETURN TO SANCTUARY` without requesting credentials. (3) Physical Android mobile performance and World Chat touch QA; record actual two-user acceptance only after it happens. Do not touch other product streams.

## GAME DUPLICATE-ACCOUNT SOCKET REPLACEMENT RECOVERY — NO FAKE PEERS (2026-11-18)

- **Starting baseline:** [Game PR #644](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/644) merged `8aa0347bc880b8683e9596c64ef61995c7bbbbc2`. Its touch Return to Sanctuary control was [successfully deployed on Pages #44295836299](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295836299). PR-head Gateway [#44295594213](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295594213), Validate [#44295594209](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295594209), Browser Smoke [#44295594221](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295594221), Hero [#44295594229](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295594229), Rarity GLB [#44295594223](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295594223) all SUCCESS. [PR-head Jarrell gameplay smoke #44295594280](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295594280) and [postmerge Game smoke #44295837115](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44295837115) still IN PROGRESS at this checkpoint.
- **New source-verified root for endless disconnect loops:** Game Worker code in `game/multiplayer/worker/index.mjs` intentionally permits only **one concurrent WebSocket per verified Supabase user ID**. When another browser connects under the same UUID, the prior socket is closed with code `4001` and reason `replaced-by-same-account`. The browser previously treated code 4001 as an ordinary transient disconnect, automatically reconnecting after 5 seconds, which then replaces the other browser, causing a perpetual room eviction cycle. Different Google/email/X sign-in methods do **not guarantee** different user IDs if provider identities are linked; this may explain some failed two-browser acceptance, but no owner account evidence yet proves that was their case.
- **Game client branch fix:** In `game/multiplayer/client-presence.js`, recognize the Worker's exact WebSocket close code **4001** and report `MULTIPLAYER · SAME ACCOUNT IN ANOTHER TAB · USE TWO DIFFERENT ACCOUNTS`. Immediately remove old real peer visuals and World Chat, stop all *automatic* retry/eviction loops for this account, and provide an explicit `RETRY ROOM CONNECTION` touch button for after the other tab is closed. A signed-out session or a truly different registered Supabase user ID clears this intentional block and allows normal server-verified connection again. Ordinary transient disconnects retain existing automatic recovery; no server rejection bypass, shared login token, fake online status, remote peer or chat.
- **Regression tests:** Simulate real server-specified WebSocket `onclose({code:4001})`, require truthful identity status, removal of World Chat, no 5-second evictions, no reconnection on same user's token refresh, and only an explicit retry creates a fresh ticket+socket (still no World Chat before the Worker's `spawn-accepted`). Test a switch to a *different* registered user independently recovers and sends only that user's own bearer. Game page versioned client becomes `v=20261118-same-account-6`.
- **Release status:** Branch only at this record checkpoint. **Actual live two-account / two-way World Chat acceptance remains unverified.** No account emails, UUIDs, passwords or JWTs are logged or requested. All PvP damage, loot, theft and grab remain disabled. No DEX, Swap, Launchpad or ClimbX code touched.
- **Exact next:** (1) Open Game-only PR and run Gateway/client security, Validate, browser and Jarrell's Peak smoke; fix any red tests before merge. (2) Merge and verify GitHub Pages deployment. (3) Owner opens two *truly distinct registered Supabase accounts* on Game page and checks both see `ONLINE`, reciprocal 3D peers and movement, and exchange confirmed World Chat. If `SAME ACCOUNT IN ANOTHER TAB`, the two sign-in methods resolve to the same underlying UUID: sign in with a second actual account; if another tab has been closed, `RETRY ROOM CONNECTION` is available. If any other displayed status, diagnose that precise ticket/WS/spawn stage without credentials or DevTools. (4) Verify Android touch and real GLB performance separately; document positive actual owner evidence before calling multiplayer LIVE.

## GAME ROOM SIGN-IN RECOVERY — REGISTERED SESSION 401 FIX (2026-11-18)

- **Known live acceptance blocker:** The owner's last real two-browser attempt showed no reciprocal avatars and no World Chat. Successful GitHub Pages deployments and offline Game smoke checks have NOT proven real authenticated World Chat. As of this checkpoint, registered-only public `game/multiplayer/client-config.json` remains `enabled:true` for `https://rooms.climboria.fun/`; all PvP damage, theft, grabbing and loot are disabled.
- **Source-verified recoverability gap:** The Game client receives HTTP `401` from `POST /ticket` when a previously stored Supabase access token is expired or invalid. It currently displays `SIGN IN AGAIN` and repeatedly retries the same getSession/token, even though the SDK may have a valid refresh token for the **same signed-in registered account**. This makes real legitimate login states unnecessarily manual/unrecoverable and hides World Chat until ticket admission.
- **Game-only fix in branch `game/refresh-room-auth-ticket-20261118`:** On initial room-ticket HTTP `401`, use the existing site's own Supabase `auth.refreshSession()` at most once within the existing 18-second ticket-stage deadline. Only retry a single ticket if Supabase returns a **fresh bearer token for exactly the same registered, non-anonymous user ID** and no logout/account switch intervened; retain fail-closed login-required behavior for expired/revoked sessions, changed identities, guest/anonymous accounts, absent provider SDK or unsuccessful refresh. Worker still independently verifies all JWTs; no secret or token is logged, stored or exposed in UI. Subsequent accepted socket/spawn still required before any peer or World Chat appears.
- **Regression tests:** Simulated stale first token / 401, exactly one same-account refresh, exactly one second `/ticket` with new token, then genuine server `welcome` + `spawn-accepted` required before chat UI. Separate negative tests reject anonymous, different-user and failed refresh outcomes without extra tickets, peers or chat. Existing no-refresh 401 test retains explicit login-required state.
- **Browser cache rollout:** `game/play.html` multiplayer script version advanced to `v=20261118-auth-recovery-3` to prevent older previously deployed client being reused. No DEX, Swap, Launchpad, ClimbX, Worker backends or game combat changes.
- **Verified previous changes:** [Pages deployment #44251471093](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44251471093) SUCCESS for prior reconnect/client-cache changes. [Game smoke #44250479734](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44250479734) SUCCESS; current [Game smoke #44251203275](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44251203275) may still be running. Separate generic browser smoke #44251203267 had **external GitHub 429 / CoinGecko CORS on unrelated homepage/Swap**; do not modify other workstreams here.

## GAME MULTIPLAYER SANCTUARY REJECTION RECOVERY MERGED (2026-11-18)

- **Merged:** [Game PR #641](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/641), squash commit `a0f0d8c980e39a2821996f6aed1532d6b7b99624`. Fixed a concrete failed initial-room-spawn reconnect dead-end in `game/multiplayer/client-presence.js` without altering Game Worker auth, account identities, PvP/loot, or other workstreams.
- **Cause and fix:** Game Worker demands a real first Sanctuary spawn within 6m of `x:0,z:-30`, `0<=y<=45`. Former client displayed `PRESS 1` on rejected spawn but retained its existing socket; subsequent attempts could not happen because `scheduleConnect` refused while that socket was open. New client safely checks actual local position after registered session verification and again at WSS OPEN; postpones ticketing/connection until a real return to Sanctuary; on authoritative `invalid-initial-spawn` closes rejected WSS, removes provisional peers and retries via fresh signed-in session and one-use ticket, **never** fabricating position, peers or World Chat. A player already exploring during a disconnect may need the normal Game key **1** to return to Sanctuary.
- **Deterministic tests:** Updated first-spawn rejection test to require closing the dead socket, discard late packets and only show World Chat after a new `spawn-accepted`. New tests prohibit ticket minting while the authenticated local hero is outside Sanctuary, allow a real return and accepted spawn to create World Chat, and prevent WSS OPEN from sending a spoofed out-of-zone initial spawn if the player moved while the ticket was pending. Existing security checks maintained.
- **PR-head CI exact SHA `1db07a1ff8b6349a77372a2eacb6544439cf13b4`:** [Game Gateway #44250479702](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44250479702) **SUCCESS**, [Validate #44250479700](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44250479700) **SUCCESS**; [Jarrell's Peak gameplay smoke #44250479734](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44250479734) still **IN PROGRESS** when merged—do not call pass without final result.
- **Postmerge production rollout pending at checkpoint:** [Pages deployment #44250723314](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44250723314), [Validate #44250723598](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44250723598), [Game smoke #44250723604](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44250723604) still in progress. **Not an actual two-account browser acceptance.** The last owner's real observation was missing peer avatars and World Chat, so customer-visible functionality must remain unverified.

## GAME MULTIPLAYER SANCTUARY ADMISSION RECOVERY (2026-11-18)

- **Observed unresolved external state:** After the registered-only live beta release, owner still has not verified real reciprocal GLB peers and World Chat. Latest [Pages #44248948261](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/44248948261) completed SUCCESS and production source config is `schema:1,enabled:true,endpoint:https://rooms.climboria.fun/`. Real user two-account acceptance remains unverified.
- **Concrete source bug found:** Isolated Game Worker `JarrellRoom` rejects `spawn` if local position is more than 6m from Sanctuary center `x=0,z=-30` or Y outside 0..45, returning `invalid-initial-spawn`. The browser client previously handled rejection with status text **only**, leaving its now unusable WebSocket open; `scheduleConnect()` refused to act while `socket` existed. The on-screen instruction to press 1 could never actually resume admission, hence no server-verified `spawn-accepted`, peers or World Chat. This is a proven source-code dead end, not yet proof it is the sole cause of owner's browser failure.
- **Game-only fix in this branch:** Check real position against the same strict initial Sanctuary rules **after real registered session verification and before minting a ticket**. For remote explorers already outside the spawn zone, show `RETURN TO SANCTUARY · PRESS 1` and retry the connection without consuming a ticket until they physically return. Recheck actual position when the WSS ticket opens in case player moved while ticket was issued; close unusable WSS without spoofing position. On server `invalid-initial-spawn`, close the rejected WSS, clear any provisional peers, and schedule fresh registered ticket/WS admission after return, preserving worker-owned spoof/teleport protections. Real user identity, server admission, 32-peer capacity, World Chat server echo and cosmetic-only attacks untouched; loot/PvP damage/grab remain OFF.
- **New regression tests:** (1) Out-of-Sanctuary player consumes **zero** initial room tickets/WS, returns, then receives a real `spawn-accepted` and *only then* World Chat. (2) Moving while a real ticket request is pending cannot transmit an invalid/spoofed spawn; later real Sanctuary return can reconnect. (3) Server rejects initial spawn; old socket is closed, stale acceptance does not create chat; fresh WebSocket and its *own accepted spawn* are required. Existing security unit suite must remain green.
- **Current status:** BRANCH ONLY pending PR/exact-head CI; no claim of live production fix or actual owner two-browser acceptance yet. Game scope only.
- **Next:** (1) Open Game-only PR, confirm gateway, Validate and gameplay smoke all SUCCESS on latest head; fix tests as needed. (2) Merge, verify Pages SUCCESS and postmerge status. (3) Owner refreshes two **registered** Game sessions on `https://climboria.fun/game/play.html`; if already exploring when joining, press **1** to return to Sanctuary (only regular gameplay control). Check both `ONLINE`, real 3D GLBs, movement sync and World Chat. (4) If still failing, use visible `MULTIPLAYER` status to target authenticated ticket/WS stage without any credentials or downloads; finish physical Android FPS/GLB/chat QA. Pass the owner-approved Player B's original registered ID (held in private process RAM, never printed/stored) to `testTwoPlayers`; after the temporary browser returns, require the identical ID, real accepted room admission/roster on both pages, and a newly attached **scene-visible remote GLB** in each page. Failure exits at the existing fixed, sanitized `disconnect-rejoin` stage. The approved QA launcher and Game runtime (client, room Worker, public config) are unchanged; no credentials or sessions enter GitHub CI.

## GAME MULTIPLAYER ADMISSION GATE — PREVENT PROVISIONAL 3D GHOST PLAYERS (2026-11-18)

- **Source-reviewed issue:** In the released `game/multiplayer/client-presence.js`, receiving the first server `welcome` packet immediately called `addPlayer()` for the provisional peer roster, and later `player-joined` could also allocate 3D peers before `spawn-accepted`. The room protocol explicitly makes the player's **own server-verified spawn-accepted** the admission boundary; a welcome proves only that the short-lived ticket was consumed, not that the player's terrain spawn was valid. This could show ghost/provisional 3D peers during a rejected or timed-out spawn despite World Chat controls still being hidden.
- **Game-only fix:** Defer rendering **all** remote peers until the authoritative `spawn-accepted` roster; do not apply joined/moved/left/punched/world-chat or duel messages to provisional/unspawned clients. Ignore late/replayed `welcome` after the verified own spawn instead of clearing a real accepted room. Keep existing 12-second admission timer, reconnection handling, zero fake peers before verified own spawn, and server-authoritative combat/loot denial unchanged. The Game Worker, asset GLBs and public client config are untouched.
- **Regression tests:** Update `game/multiplayer/client-presence.test.mjs` tests that formerly *expected* provisional ghosts. Add a new test: `welcome` with a real-looking peer and `player-joined`/`player-moved`/`player-punched`/`world-chat` packets all must render **zero** remote avatars before accepted spawn; a valid accepted roster then adds exactly one real peer; a late welcome must not clear that peer. The malformed/mismatched own `spawn-accepted` and timeout/reconnect tests must remain green. This is deterministic simulated Worker traffic, **not** actual owner-operated two-account QA.
- **Expected CI / evidence (pending this PR):** Game Authenticated Room Gateway (No Live PvP) with client-presence tests, Validate Climboria and Jarrell's Peak gameplay smoke. Any triggered separate browser/hero/chest checks must also pass before merge. Record exact head/merge SHA, PR links and actual CI run URLs/conclusions after verification; do not claim completed or deployed merely because the code was written.
- **Exact next steps:** (1) Review and run triggered Game-only CI, fix legitimate failures, merge only when all required jobs are successful, then record SHA/run links here. (2) Owner downloads latest reviewed Windows `Climboria_Game_QA_Kit`, signs into two **distinct registered non-anonymous** accounts interactively (no credentials in chat/CI), and records sanitized actual accepted room spawn, remote GLB/map appearance, reciprocal keyboard movement, server-echo World Chat, disconnect/rejoin. (3) Separately record real Android GLB/chat/frame rate acceptance, then consider a separately reviewed public multiplayer enablement PR. **Current Game production multiplayer remains OFF** (`schema:1`, `enabled:false`, `endpoint:null`); PvP damage/theft/grab/loot OFF. No DEX, Swap, Launchpad or ClimbX changes.

## GAME TWO-BROWSER QA — REJECT LOADED-BUT-HIDDEN REMOTE GLBS (2026-11-18)

- **Verified baseline:** The merged Game-only [PR #630](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/630) and [PR #631](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/631) already delivered the portable six-file QA kit and durable checkpoint. Their release gate remains **NO-GO**: actual two registered non-anonymous Game users have **not** passed owner-operated WebGL World Chat/movement + physical Android QA. Public `game/multiplayer/client-config.json` remains `enabled:false` and `endpoint:null`. No need to repeat previously successful cloud readiness and unauthorized ticket checks.
- **Concrete gap found by source inspection:** the owner-operated `qa-mixed-login.mjs` remote-avatar check previously passed on `audit.resident>=1` alone. A remote GLB might be loaded but detached from the active world scene or hidden, so CI/owner logs could incorrectly say a remote 3D avatar was visible. A name ring/map marker alone also must not count as the remote GLB.
- **Code change (Game only):** add a **read-only** `sceneVisibleResidents` count to `window.__CLIMBORIA_GAME_PEERS.audit()` inside `game/play.html`. It counts only actual remote GLB roots still attached to their corresponding peer groups, with both root and group marked visible and group attached to the Game scene. Tighten `game/multiplayer/worker/qa-mixed-login.mjs` to require both `resident===1` and `sceneVisibleResidents===1` in **each** of the two authenticated browser windows before its real-avatar stage can pass. In the existing Jarrell's Peak browser smoke `.github/scripts/game-smoke.mjs`, assert the fake **marker-only** local renderer exercise registers zero `sceneVisibleResidents` before, during and after cleanup. This tests diagnostics against fake positive avatar reports; it is **not** a real network peer.
- **Scope and limitations:** The check confirms active scene membership and visibility flags, **not actual camera frustum/pixel appearance or physical Android FPS**. It cannot replace human visual confirmation. No change to room Worker, authentication, remote player spawning, gameplay PvP damage/grabbing/theft/loot, live public release configuration, Swap/DEX/Launchpad/ClimbX. `game/multiplayer/worker/STAGING_READINESS.md` updated with correct proof boundaries.
- **Next exact steps:** (1) Review and run all triggered Game CI checks, including Jarrell's Peak desktop/mobile smoke, Game Gateway, Validate Climboria and portable QA kit rebuild; fix errors if any. (2) Merge Game-only PR **only after** all required checks reach SUCCESS and record PR SHA and CI run links. (3) Use latest GitHub kit for local owner-approved QA with two genuinely different registered Supabase sessions; record actual distinct sign-in, server-accepted spawns, visible in-scene GLB, map, keyboard movement, bidirectional World Chat, disconnection/rejoin and actual Android device FPS. (4) Public multiplayer stays OFF without positive real-account + device evidence. Do not imply that offline smoke proves live two-user gameplay.

## LIVE GAME ROOM NEGATIVE AUTH / ORIGIN SECURITY PROBES — PR CHECKS PENDING (2026-11-18)

- **Reason/limits:** Owner wants continued Game multiplayer readiness work without manually creating AI-owned test accounts or handling credentials. The registered-account Game WebGL acceptance gate is **still unverified**. Do not create a fake Supabase account, send real JWTs to CI, or imply that a rejected unauthorized request proves a successful signed-in multiplayer session.
- **Implemented:** `game/multiplayer/worker/qa-access-denial-check.mjs` runs exactly two bounded, credential-free requests against only `https://rooms.climboria.fun/ticket`: (1) a POST with the first-party `Origin: https://climboria.fun` but NO Authorization; require **401** and exact JSON `{"error":"login-required"}` with trusted-origin CORS, and (2) a POST with `Origin: https://invalid-origin.invalid` and NO Authorization; require **403** and `{"error":"origin-not-allowed"}` with no CORS permission. The deployed `game/multiplayer/worker/index.mjs` checks Origin at the gateway entry and missing bearer before reading the body, contacting Supabase or issuing a ticket. These negative requests carry only `{}` and must never issue a usable ticket, connect a WebSocket or touch customer identity. Strict response status, bounded JSON, no redirects, timeout and generic redacted failure logs are enforced.
- **Offline tests:** `game/multiplayer/worker/qa-access-denial-check.test.mjs` covers the exact allowed hostname and ordered denied requests, no credentials, forged success, wrong status/body/CORS, unavailable network and invalid deadlines. The existing Game Gateway `node --test game/multiplayer/worker/*.test.mjs` covers these tests. Offline PASS is not live deployment evidence.
- **Live CI (this PR):** dedicated Game workflow file `.github/workflows/game-room-live-readiness-readonly.yml`, now named **GAME Room Readiness + Unauthorized Denials (No Secrets)**, continues the existing public-config fail-closed + real `/health`/`/ready` checks and adds the two negative live staging probes. Trigger: relevant Game-only PR or manual workflow dispatch. GitHub job permissions: `contents: read`; no tokens, no Supabase credentials, no Worker deployment or public gameplay enablement. **Actual live 401/403 checks are not claimed PASS until this PR's workflow completes successfully.**
- **Exact next steps:** (1) Require successful Game Gateway, repository validation, Jarrell’s Peak gameplay smoke and this live Game-readiness/denial workflow; record run URLs, exact PR/merge SHA, and any failed assertion (without response bodies or user tokens). (2) Squash merge Game-only PR once safe and update this record. ## PLAYER SOCKET OPEN + VERIFIED SERVER SPAWN ADMISSION — MERGED WITH 3/3 EXACT-HEAD CI (2026-11-16)

- **Game-only [PR #565](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/565) MERGED:** exact reviewed/tested PR HEAD **`49804d8c81b188864ba23c1ed3061650c43e6e57`**, squash-merged into main as **`4a38700eeb9f96a8281e31730ffb621a777a7f10`**. PR changed only `game/multiplayer/client-presence.js`, `game/multiplayer/client-presence.test.mjs`, and this Game report; no Game client-config, cloud Worker/DO deployment, Game art, DEX, Swap, Launchpad, ClimbX, wallet, token, real PvP damage or loot changes. The concurrent main commits changed only DEX/Swap files, and PR mergeability was verified clean immediately before exact-head merge.
- **EXACT-HEAD CI all 3 TERMINAL SUCCESS before merge:** [Game Authenticated Room Gateway #43835781447](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43835781447), [Validate Climboria #43835781521](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43835781521), [Jarrell's Peak full desktop/mobile Gameplay Smoke #43835781557](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43835781557). Gateway job logs confirmed **82/82** security tests PASS plus **27/27** browser-player VM tests PASS, including four new admission timeout/logout/rejoin regressions. These tests are **not evidence** of real Cloudflare staging, two registered QA users or physical Android FPS.
- **Source-level issue fixed:** Earlier authentic `getSession()` had an independent 8-second deadline and ticket fetch+JSON had an 18-second deadline, but new WebSocket `open` or first valid `spawn-accepted` could hang indefinitely. `armAdmissionTimer` now sets **12 seconds** for WebSocket open, resets **12 seconds** after open for verified server spawn acknowledgement. `open` and `welcome` alone never show ONLINE. On expiry client evicts and closes stale socket, clears provisional peers/chat/duel state, displays RECONNECTING and retries a fresh verified ticket after **5 seconds** even if old `onclose` never arrives. On matched valid `spawn-accepted`, socket close, logout or account switch the deadline is cancelled. Epoch and exact socket checks prevent late old packets from resurrecting remote peers. Source commit `d9b1dc0347a6852e2a426bdced2aafc38fe5d42b`; regression test commit `504735e75c24be4174795f6b67ce9653c776de4e`; final documented/tested branch head `49804d8c81b188864ba23c1ed3061650c43e6e57`.
- **Release state:** `game/multiplayer/client-config.json` independently read on main, still **`enabled:false`, `endpoint:null`**. Source merge verified; postmerge Pages deployment not yet separately checked. The Game Worker and Durable Object, live `/ready` 200 backed by DO storage, two real separate registered/non-anonymous Supabase QA browsers with synced remote GLBs/movement/World Chat/reconnect, physical Android FPS and owner production approval remain **unverified**. - **Source bug:** `game/multiplayer/worker/index.mjs` `readBoundedJSON(req)` enforced UTF-8 body <=1024 bytes but awaited `reader.read()` without an independent deadline. A slow client can send no bytes or one incomplete JSON chunk and hold a ticket request forever. The original oversized-body and parse-error paths also `await reader.cancel()`, which itself may never resolve on a malicious/stalled stream. This is a source-level guard gap, **not a demonstrated live exploit or deployed Game outage**.
- **Focused Game-only branch:** `game/bounded-room-json-request-body-2026-11-16`. Small inbound JSON body parser now enforces **5s entire read-and-parse wall-clock deadline**, in addition to the existing Content-Length and actual streamed 1,024-byte cap. At timeout it attempts stream cancellation without awaiting a blocked cancel promise; returns `null` to existing public `400 invalid-request` fail-closed handler. Malformed UTF-8, exceeded bytes, unclosed/truncated streams remain rejected. No Supabase auth/DO ticket claim occurs for invalid body; legacy valid JSON and 30s one-use tickets remain intact. This is independent of 6.5s auth stage / 3.5s DO issuance deadlines in #547; no guarantee is made of a single whole-request bound from external network through WebSocket.
- **Regression tests:** Game Worker Node suite adds a never-ending empty request stream, an incomplete JSON chunk then stall, both with intentionally unresolved `cancel()`, and a 1,200-byte body whose cancellation promise hangs. Uses a short test-only mock of the exact 5s deadline and separate real watchdog; requires bounded `400 invalid-request` and stream cancellation attempt without any auth/DO ticket calls. Existing normal valid JSON, 401 invalid JWT, 429 capacity and authenticated room code remain unchanged. `STAGING_READINESS.md` documents owner expectations. These are offline simulations only, not staged two-player acceptance.

## WORLD CHAT REQUEST-LEVEL ACK CORRELATION — DRAFT GAME-ONLY CHANGE (2026-11-16)

- **Baseline:** Game PR #536 merged with 3/3 exact-head PASS, squash SHA `90bfc125b2e3f1002822cc72d6a141fed3f56fa9`. Its real two-account WebGL chat/GLB acceptance remains **owner-only and NOT EXECUTED** because Cloudflare Game Worker staging and registered QA accounts are not confirmed. Game public multiplayer is still `enabled:false` and `endpoint:null`.
- **Bug discovered in merged source:** PR #531 correlates a pending World Chat send via authenticated `selfId` + text only. If an 8-second unconfirmed send is manually retried with **the exact same text**, a late server echo from the older attempt can falsely acknowledge the newer pending message. Likewise a delayed cooldown rejection from an earlier attempt may unlock an unrelated newer pending send. Existing server broadcast did not provide a request identifier. This is not evidence of fake players; it is a reliable delivery-status ambiguity.
- **Game-only feature branch:** `game/world-chat-confirmation-sequence-2026-11-16`. Browser assigns a bounded, ascending `chatSeq` to each actual World Chat send. Only an authenticated self-echo that matches **both normalized text and exact `chatSeq`** can clear that same still-matching draft. Worker validates optional `chatSeq` as positive integer <= 2,147,483,647 and copies it only from the authenticated sending socket into the broadcast echo or associated cooldown/invalid-text rejection, **never allowing client-provided player identity, HP or payment fields**. Legacy chat packets without sequence are still displayed/broadcast for older/manual tests, but cannot falsely acknowledge newer sequenced client input. Late self-echo/rejection after timeout cannot clear or unlock a newer send.
- **Regressions:** Client VM tests cover own echo sequence, normalized Unicode, same-text retry after timeout, stale old echo, stale rejection and no duplicate in-flight send. Worker unit tests assert valid echoed sequence on both peers, server rejection sequence for cooldown/invalid text, and fail-closed invalid/negative/oversized/noninteger correlation field. `e2e-two-accounts.mjs` owner-only network acceptance now checks an actual server echo sequence in both browsers. `STAGING_READINESS.md` reinforces exact-sequence real two-account QA. These tests run on CI synthetic Worker/VM; **no live registered two-player staging claim**.
- **Source commits:** client `a8723be4`, Worker `5923e57a` (corrected early invalid-seq branch), client VM tests `13d367c2`, Worker tests `ede3f1f7`, manual two-account sequence QA `09c44709`, staging checklist `f881188c`. PR/CI/merge **not yet verified** at this draft checkpoint.

## GAME ROOM TICKET-FLOOD SECURITY HARDENING — PRE-MERGE (2026-11-15)

- **Root cause confirmed from Game Worker source:** `JarrellRoom.fetch('/_ticket')` formerly counted at most 32 outstanding 30-second tickets **globally**, without a per-verified-Supabase-account pending-ticket cap. Any authenticated malicious registered account could request 32 unconsumed tickets repeatedly, receive `room-full` for all others, and deny legitimate players access to multiplayer/World Chat. No deployed exploit observed; release remains disabled.
- **Game-only branch `game/room-account-ticket-flood-protection-2026-11-15`:** `game/multiplayer/worker/index.mjs` now prunes expired tickets and **atomically** checks current room capacity, all outstanding tickets and at most **2 unconsumed tickets per verified Supabase user** via Durable Object `state.storage.transaction`. A third ticket gets 429 `account-ticket-limit`, no ticket inserted. Unique 64-hex one-time ticket cannot overwrite an existing pending ticket (409 `duplicate-ticket`). Global pending-32, live peers-32, one-time consume, 30s expiry, Supabase verified JWT and anonymous-guest denial remain. Two outstanding allow normal brief browser reconnects; expired tickets automatically release slots. No client-controlled identity, PvP damage/loot or wallet logic added.
- **Automated regression tests:** `game/multiplayer/worker/index.test.mjs` models serialized concurrent requests from one account (2 allowed, third denied), confirms other account still receives ticket, duplicate ticket denied without overwrite, expired account slot freed, active per-account cap preserved and 33rd distinct user blocked by global pending room capacity. `STAGING_READINESS.md` now documents the real 429 behavior. Real staging will still require two distinct registered users.
- **Status:** changes committed to Game-only branch; exact-head Gateway, Validate and full Jarrell Gameplay CI **NOT VERIFIED YET**. Public `game/multiplayer/client-config.json` still `enabled:false,endpoint:null`. No owner-authorized Cloudflare deployment or two-account GLB/map/World Chat/mobile FPS proof, no edits to DEX, Swap, Launchpad, ClimbX, token or wallet.
- **EXACT NEXT (Game only):** open Game-only draft PR, inspect changed filenames and public config, wait for all exact-head Game Gateway, Validate and full Gameplay Smoke checks. Fix and retest any failure; merge only after full success and record merge SHA + postmerge checks. Owner then establishes protected GitHub `game-room-staging` environment with scoped Cloudflare token/account ID, existing Supabase publishable key, manually approves isolated Game Worker staging deployment, maps `rooms.climboria.fun`, checks DO `/ready`, tests actual TWO registered independent Supabase browser sessions for animated real GLB peers, map markers, bidirectional World Chat, attack visual, sprint movement, logout/cleanup and physical Android FPS. Separate public enable PR ONLY after explicit owner acceptance.

## GAME SPAWN-VERIFIED MULTIPLAYER / WORLD CHAT — REVIEW (2026-11-15)

- **Prior verify:** PR #488 merged, postmerge Gameplay #43692637375, Validate #43692637348, and Pages #43692637301 all **SUCCESS**. Pages is a static deployment, NOT a real room Worker.

- **Game-only branch:** `game/spawn-verified-presence-2026-11-15`. Pending authenticated sessions now consume room capacity but remain invisible to snapshots, join/leave broadcasts, and other clients until a server-validated spawn. New `spawn-accepted` ack opens World Chat/DUELS in browser only after a trusted matching session ID; unaccepted spawn reports RETURN TO SANCTUARY. Existing core room behavior is backward-compatible unless the Worker explicitly opts into deferred visibility.
- **Concurrent-join race also fixed:** a socket pending its own spawn could miss another peer's join while broadcasts were intentionally hidden, so each `spawn-accepted` now contains a complete *current verified peer roster*. Browser reconciles additions, departures and position updates (without duplicate GLB models); real two-account E2E checks this roster. No fabricated peers are added.
- **Tests updated:** `presence-room.test.mjs` pending peers and no ghost join/leave, `worker/index.test.mjs` invalid spawn and accepted spawn, `client-presence.test.mjs` chat/duel gating, and real two-account `worker/e2e-two-accounts.mjs` waits for server spawn confirmations. - **Status now:** branch committed, exact-head CI **NOT YET VERIFIED**; public `game/multiplayer/client-config.json` stays `enabled:false`, `endpoint:null`. No Game Worker or real two-account staging has been deployed/tested. No DEX, Swap, Launchpad, ClimbX, wallet, loot or real damage modifications.

## GAME ROOM WORKER VALID LENGTH HOTFIX — numeric header regression (2026-11-15 PHT)

- **Game-only security/availability fix** on branch `game/worker-content-length-fix-2026-11-15`, forked from verified Game `main` SHA `c6484dac1017fc91573ac2ed7f5c6edda312bd44`. Original bounded request reader accidentally used the JavaScript regular expression literal `/^\\d+$/` (TWO actual backslashes before `d`) to validate the incoming HTTP `Content-Length`; it matches the literal sequence `\\d`, not decimal digits. Thus a legitimate numeric `Content-Length` header could be denied with a 400 before Supabase auth. The existing malformed-header, chunk size and UTF-8 overflow tests did not cover this ordinary valid-header case.
- **Fix commit `784f3c7e1f6ecc5f6a583ce6f02e2f87b4d6363d`:** use one regex escape `/^\d+$/` to match real nonnegative ASCII numeric lengths. Keep the independent read-stream <=1024 UTF-8 **wire bytes** budget, early cancel on overflow, strict UTF-8 decoder, malformed length rejection, allowed Origin, Supabase auth and one-use WebSocket ticket security unchanged.
- **Regression commit `1d6c35105a71e81345cd5ee8756ea0c0adc09d6a`:** a real Node Request with correctly declared decimal Content-Length and valid JSON passes the trusted Supabase-account verification path and issues exactly one room ticket. Existing malformed/oversized denial tests remain.
- **Status:** only Game Worker code, its Node unit tests and this Game record changed. **NOT YET verified by final exact-head Game Gateway/Validate/Gameplay CI; no deployment.** The Game room client remains `enabled:false`, `endpoint:null`. This hotfix is independent from in-flight Game duel consent PR #482 and dependent E2E PR #483; do not let those branches later overwrite this fixed Worker file.
- **EXACT NEXT:** open Game-only draft PR, require exact-head Gateway/Validate and any full Jarrell Gameplay CI, review diff and merge only if checks pass. Then reconcile PR #482 against updated `main` preserving BOTH this numeric-header fix and new authenticated consent signaling, and retarget #483 after parent merge. Owner still has to approve isolated Cloudflare Game staging/two real accounts; do not infer online gameplay or PvP from static Pages success. Preserve DEX/Swap/Launchpad/ClimbX.

## GAME AUTHENTICATED DUEL CONSENT SIGNALING — default OFF (2026-11-15 PHT)

- **Scope and release truth:** Game-only branch `game/duel-consent-signaling-2026-11-15` based on last verified `main` `c6484dac1017fc91573ac2ed7f5c6edda312bd44`. No DEX, Swap, Launchpad, ClimbX, wallet, loot or production deployment changes. `game/multiplayer/client-config.json` stays `enabled:false` and `endpoint:null` until owner explicitly deploys isolated Worker staging and verifies two real accounts. Existing server-side `attack` remains REJECTED.
- **New Game-only Worker signaling**, commits `f0a94b0cc5c257115b758ac462f876d1148669a9` and `7a1740951a70e4ec28100097a0529584faa78302`: WebSocket session-bound `duel-request`, `duel-response` (explicit boolean accept/decline), `duel-cancel`. Invitation is addressed only to an authenticated connected, spawned `s_<32hex>` peer ID. The server never trusts client `actorId`, health, damage, wallet, inventory, or account ID. Responses carry `combatDamageEnabled:false`; no player health modification via websocket and no imported `ServerCombatState.attack` call. Successful opt-in is a staged ephemeral room signal, **not playable PvP**. A failed recipient delivery revokes consent instead of falsely acknowledging acceptance.
- **Duel-state hardening**, commits `dad474211704ead8d392ef3ab9e555301184f3d6` and `77c6992c6171789a621d1ad82cc158d2998166fe`: consent cancellation clears incoming/outgoing invites and active HP/opponent pairing without bypassing the 5s invite anti-spam clock; server disconnect/session replacement clears state even if presence membership was already removed; prohibit cyclic simultaneous inbound/outbound invitations. Existing 30s invite TTL, per-session rate limiting, Sanctuary protection and server-trusted-position default null remain intact.
- **Tests committed**, `b9f4d92efeb75ac167dee2db125667d4dbde24a9` adds two focused tests for cycle prevention, opt-out/cooldown and disconnect-before-cleanup; `997d6b5241794ad92345ff43184bc06feff4c170` extends actual mock-socket authenticated two-peer protocol, verifies safe-area rejection, normal invitation and response, forged ID/damage ignored, explicit cancellation, unchanged 500 HP, still-rejected client ATTACK and invitation cooldown. Existing Worker abuse/replay and authenticated ticket tests remain unchanged.
- **Caveats:** No Game client invitation UI or consent-modal wiring yet; source is in the disabled future Cloudflare Game Worker, not live. The room's visual positions still come from client messages and **must never authorize attacks**; any future actual HP damage requires a newly audited trusted terrain verifier, consent revocation and safe-zone design decision. This does not solve independent two-account staging, hosting/secrets or mobile GLB/FPS.
- **Exact next tests and workflow:** open a Game-only draft PR targeting `main`. Require exact-head Gateway, Server Authority, Validate and Jarrell Gameplay runs, fix real failures without bypassing checks, verify three/four/five-file Game-only diff and default-off config. Perform manual two-account staging only with explicitly approved isolated Cloudflare Worker secrets; inspect role-based invitation/cancel/reconnect and player model FPS; build a usable consent dialog separately before considering public release. Document tested run IDs/commit and never call a GitHub CI success proof of production PvP. [Multiplayer release gate #479](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/issues/479), [Meshy import #466](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/466).

## GAME CSS ROOT CAUSE — mobile ATTACK must NEVER appear on keyboard desktop (2026-11-15)

- **Reproduced true regression on 1280px desktop in exact-head Chest CI #43678544536:** `GAME device-input-mode desktop {"viewport":1280,"touchPoints":0,"coarse":false,"touchHandlerPresent":false,"bodyClass":"free","controlDisplay":"grid","controlWidth":68}`. Although all touch detectors correctly reported desktop and body lacked `mobile-device`, circular ATTACK button still showed. The Chest QA diagnostic commit `d2ccbc9aa76f26c388fcf9352405e8de11e4021a` failed deliberately for this real UI bug.
- **Root cause:** stylesheet had `body.free [data-mobile-action]{display:grid!important;...}` applying to **all devices** and overriding the button's `display:none` even on keyboard-only desktop. This explains why modifying JS touch detection alone did not help.
- **Actual fix `55d884c65dc3f0fba6886d0a93808f7cd0895453`:** only `body.free.mobile-device [data-mobile-action]` gets forced visible. Desktop remains `display:none`; mobile continues to show five correctly-sized non-overlapping touch actions and centered ATTACK label.
- **Verification:** new exact-head Game Chest test must pass with desktop `controlDisplay:none` and mobile visible, and full Jarrell Gameplay must preserve Q key attack, real authored GLB clip, cooldown/stamina/no fake damage plus desktop/mobile screenshots. Do NOT merge until all 5 required exact-head checks succeed. The original merged Game Worker security PR #480 must be included in main reconciliation before release.
- **Scope:** Game-only. Do not activate Game multiplayer (client still default-off), nonconsensual PvP or unverified Meshy art; never change DEX, Swap, Launchpad or ClimbX.

## GAME DESKTOP INPUT MODE — ATTACK button hidden on keyboard-only PCs (2026-11-15)

- **Visual QA found actual desktop bug:** Game Rarity Chest desktop Playwright capture at 1280×720 (context `hasTouch:false`) showed a floating mobile ATTACK circle. Original detection `('ontouchstart' in window)||navigator.maxTouchPoints>0` misidentified a desktop with an exposed event-handler property as a touch device. The user requires desktop **Q key ATTACK**, not mobile touch overlay.
- **Fix** `f7dcf3eb4bd12023d2a228fd9585cff0e459b511`: initial guard used actual `navigator.maxTouchPoints>0` or CSS `(pointer:coarse)`. Subsequent exact-head 1280×720 desktop Chest screenshot still showed the floating ATTACK circle, so final fix `2209424296e14a89f44a05d56a1bf126fd6e1206` **requires actual `navigator.maxTouchPoints>0`** and ignores coarse-pointer hints. This preserves real mobile/touch laptops and keeps keyboard-only desktop UI clear; final exact-head screenshots/tests still required.
- **Regression** `fe4d70b00148a7aa12e58c77fb2b81cccba36c0d` (follow-up `6b800b814849f89e009f575f308d818e7d738a2a`): real-browser Desktop Gameplay Smoke explicitly checks `touchPoints===0`, pointer not coarse, `body.mobile-device` absent and touch ATTACK button computed `display:none`; existing mobile tests still require visible, centered, non-overlapping ATTACK button and existing Q key attack/state checks.
- **Current branch** Game-only draft PR #477; no merge or deployment yet, no claim of CI pass after these changes. **EXACT NEXT:** run all five exact-head Validate, Browser, Hero, Chest, full Jarrell Gameplay tests; inspect true desktop/mobile screenshots, verify no unintended loss of Android touch controls, then merge only after all pass and record final main SHA and Pages deployment evidence.
- **Other Game workstreams:** PR #478 server-owned consent/damage core remains default-deny and unstaged; PR #480 fixes request body streaming limit in the still-off Game Worker, and owner Meshy binaries remain blocked in PR #466. No non-Game modules touched.

## Priority change — Hero creator before gameplay (2026-11-14, PHT)

**Owner decision:** The **Create Hero card belongs in the Expedition/Game hub**. Players must not start Jarrell's Peak without first creating a hero. Players can edit a saved name, see a complete 3D live preview, and enter only when their hero is valid and saved. The GLB is a bundled official model, **not a required file upload**. This task takes priority over earlier gameplay performance work; Arena remains planned.

### Actual code changes, committed on `game/hero-creation-gate-2026-11-14`
- `game/index.html` — added independent hero creation card and accessible editor modal, 3D full-body model loaded from `../assets/characters/Climboria_Hero.glb`, rotate/zoom preview, name input/validation, local browser save/edit and launch-gate navigation. No additional world destination added. Commit `047130789462cace299a44b57d484c82706e52de`, follow-up loader fix `ed36d257bdbac21e75eeeb25c71f6aded657b7d2`, preview reopen and pinch fix `c725e8815d615d8e905ac045143969a5fb328520`.
- `game/play.html` — direct URL preflight requires a valid locally saved hero; denies world setup otherwise, returns to menu, and displays saved hero name on in-game HUD. Commits `726019d1f2cb948346dc7d5544f02c52817abf3e` and `8dc057fad114fbce5898808435c31ecba463cac1`.
- `.github/scripts/game-smoke.mjs` — negative test for anonymous direct-world entry, valid desktop/mobile local hero fixtures, and menu card gating coverage. Commit `2a4166f1ac0702aea28029385537ada157f52f69`.
- `docs/GAME_DEVELOPMENT_RECORD.md` — updated durable task handoff on the active branch; commit SHA on GitHub file history.

### Important technical findings
- Prior `game/index.html` linked Create Hero to `game/character-lab.html`, a standalone *developer GLB upload tester* with no player-creation persistence; existing launch click directly navigated to `play.html` without hero validation.
- Runtime `game/play.html` already loads official `../assets/characters/Climboria_Hero.glb`; uploading a model from a user's computer is not needed.
- Current hero name saved as `climboria-created-hero-v1` in localStorage (model `Adventurer`). This is **local-only** and can be cleared or manually edited by browser users. It is *not secure account-level identity or anti-cheat validation*.
- The new model preview uses the existing Three.js and GLTFLoader distribution; on slow devices, the 42.9 MB GLB is a performance concern. Loading and full-body framing require real browser verification.
- `game/character-lab.html` remains an independent developer test utility, not part of player create-hero flow.

### Death-loot recovery — new Game-only branch (2026-11-14 PHT)
- **Prior completed milestone:** Create Hero standing A-pose, mandatory game entry, live original 42.9 MB GLB preview, identity HUD, sprint drain/recovery merged to `main` in [PR #297](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/297) at merge commit `772841cc3b710a1386aadb63848483669818f7b0`. - **Gameplay change:** On fatal damage, carried 50-slot local backpack drops into one visible 3D loot bag at the death coordinates for **180 seconds**, player respawns at Sanctuary, and approaches/retrieves their own bag using **E / mobile USE** at ~4 meters. HUD prompt shows remaining time and distance. Recovered stacks restore to the local inventory and are stored; expired bags are destroyed. A new death carrying other loot replaces the old bag. If backpack has too many occupied slots, recovery is blocked rather than silently losing remaining items. This is **single-player local-only**, not actual multiplayer looting or secure item ownership.
- **Implemented commits:** `f6a358318e005c48112d65c5ae10d1f9ba9ebb99` (drop/recover/3-minute expiry); `f58c07cd8c3ac3834e5646b222f7d1c33b6575a5` (near/far HUD prompts); `4ff0d7dd9f2016f29fdd51b232e58c02dc4c2649` (death-to-recovery smoke audit); `7b3ef009f2125f96d61dcc157795f3e71b0d25ed` (Playwright lifecycle assertions).
- **Reload reliability gap identified and resolved:** The initial bag was memory-only, so refresh erased the recoverable loot. Commit `56d20b45409e8e36629cdae2dc00a55674681495` now persists only valid, unexpired bag metadata in localStorage, keyed to the same guest/account scope as the backpack. On reload, reconstruct a bag mesh; invalid/expired records are cleared. On identity changes, unload previous bag without deleting that identity's stored item data; load only the new identity's bag. Commit `3697667b5dcd4b00a4977a6f8dc06553b095015d` tests persisted serialization, unload/reload, guest/account key isolation, pickup, expiry cleanup, and correct inventory restoration. Commit `7b36b8e225d03390ede6f44cb4a270768bab8a58` strengthens Playwright assertions.
- **Local syntax verification:** `game/play.html` inline JS (2 scripts) and `.github/scripts/game-smoke.mjs` parse successfully. This is not a substitute for desktop/mobile browser CI.
- **Release limitations:** Prototype stores loot in editable browser localStorage, not authoritative server storage. No ownership proofs, other-player looting, network custody, server expiry clock, or reconciliation guarantees. On death, current prototype treats **all backpack entries as carried loot**; permanent/equipped/insured loot rules remain an explicit design and balance decision. Before multiplayer and valuable items, implement server-side bag persistence, inventory transactions, abuse protection, and robust crash consistency. No production/live claim.

### Automatic Jarrell's Peak weather — separate map/environment branch (2026-11-14 PHT)
- **User requirement:** weather must change automatically every **15 minutes of active gameplay**, not be manually controlled in Game Settings. Hero GLB/rig/animation is explicitly out of this owner's scope until user supplies a replacement.
- **Branch:** `game/jarrell-automatic-weather-2026-11-14`, based on main independently of [coastal cave map PR #358](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/358), so it does not cancel or rewrite that PR's live CI.
- **Implemented** `484ec23afa6d3003802795730351bc3ee4045fd3` in `game/play.html`: pure `weatherPhaseStep` advances Clear → Rain → Thunderstorm → Clear exactly each 900,000 active gameplay milliseconds, with elapsed-cycle preservation across reload in localStorage; offline/browser-closed time is not incorrectly counted. `advanceWeather(dt)` saves every 30 seconds and on changes, with one event for weather changes. Rain and fog/sky lighting transition smoothly over seconds rather than abrupt toggling. Manual weather button removed from Expedition Settings and normal KeyR weather override disabled; optional `?debug-weather` override remains developer-only. HUD still displays current weather; time-of-day controls unchanged.
- **Regression** `f8b529f3d9bbbc6eb72125d0313f8cf2184aec77` in `.github/scripts/game-smoke.mjs` checks 899,999ms boundary, exact 900,000ms rotation, subsequent states and cycle wrap, preservation of elapsed time on resume, multi-interval catch-up, absence of normal settings control and ignored ordinary R key. No 15-minute CI wait needed.
- **Checks so far:** both inline Game JavaScript scripts parse successfully, and game Playwright smoke syntax passes. Exact-head desktop/mobile runtime CI and visual atmospheric QA still PENDING. Not merged or deployed. Do not touch DEX/Swap/Launchpad/ClimbX or the original GLB.
- **Next:** open a separate Game-only PR, run Validate/Browser/Jarrell Gameplay Smoke, fix any Game-specific failure, sync latest main without overwriting cave PR or other-team changes. Later review mobile rain/lighting quality and add environmental SFX only with properly licensed assets.

## GAME GATEWAY PAYLOAD BOUNDS — untrusted streaming body must fail closed (2026-11-15)

- **Source audit:** `game/multiplayer/worker/index.mjs` used `await req.text()` to buffer a complete unauthenticated body before checking a 1,024-character threshold, allowing unbounded transfer and UTF-8 multi-byte bypass when `Content-Length` was missing/forged. This is a Game Worker hardening issue only; the Worker is NOT deployed or live.
- **Code commit `93f5fc3e4d1d642f2f86f02833af3a36dfb7d971`:** read the Request `ReadableStream` incrementally, account actual UTF-8 wire bytes, reject invalid/oversized `Content-Length`, cancel oversized streams immediately, reject invalid encodings and malformed JSON, and preserve the existing 1,024-byte budget. Does not touch auth, room state, client game, rewards or DEX.
- **Tests commit `ea6f98081e18565b9150b72a1267fb42052a5f8f`:** add three Game gateway tests requiring fail-closed cancellation of chunked overflows without a length header, rejection of multibyte UTF-8 that fits character but not byte budget, and early rejection of invalid length headers. Existing ticket issuance, replay, authenticated identity and room Join/Attack visuals remain in existing suite.
- **CHECKPOINT STATUS:** source and tests committed on separate Game-only branch `game/room-bounded-json-2026-11-15`; exact-head Gateway, Server policy, Validate and Gameplay CI are **NOT YET VERIFIED**. This is NOT an online deploy and does not turn on `game/multiplayer/client-config.json`.
- **EXACT NEXT:** open draft Game-only PR; confirm new gateway tests on exact PR head and inspect failures/logs, fix until green. Check no impacted DEX/Swap/Launchpad/ClimbX files. Since this Game Worker remains default-off, do not expose API publicly until owner-reviewed staging secrets, actual independent two-user WebSocket validation, rate limits/room state audits and real mobile WebGL QA. Note pending ATTACK controls PR #477 and isolated consent PR #478 must each independently pass.

The server rightly rejects a >28m/s teleport as `move-too-fast`, leaving remote clients seeing a stale position/ghost and potentially blocking later legitimate updates. A local teleport must NOT be accepted as an untrusted `move` or grant damage/loot; resynchronize through fresh room admission instead.

Existing protected Sanctuary positional validation remains mandatory; moving to other waypoints does NOT auto-enter multiplayer, shows RETURN TO SANCTUARY until user chooses it.
